Type reference

micron::posix::node_types

Defined in header <micron/linux/io/inode.hpp>.

An inode-format classification enum used by metadata predicates.

Classification and queries

Values name socket, symlink, regular_file, block_device, directory, character_device, fifo, not_found, and unknown. get_type uses stat and follows symlinks; is_symlink uses lstat, and the corresponding *at predicate selects AT_SYMLINK_NOFOLLOW. Metadata failure becomes false or not_found, rather than retaining a precise error. not_found can therefore also represent access/other query failure. Queries from an existing stat record avoid another syscall.

Mode predicates test individual stored permission/special bits; is_readable/writable/executable ask access/faccessat and do not merely inspect those bits. is_virtual_file means regular file with device major 0, a heuristic. is_same_file compares device/inode. Size queries return -1 on stat failure; inode/mode/link queries use zero sentinels, uid/gid/device/block/time queries use cast -1, so distinguish these from actual field values when required.

is_empty_dir duplicates a descriptor but the duplicate shares the same open-file-description offset: its rewind and getdents therefore change the original descriptor position. Errors return false; has_content negates emptiness, so it can be true after an error. is_mountpoint compares the device id with a text-derived parent and special-cases /; it does not detect every bind mount and is not a general canonical mount resolver. Path strings must be valid and terminated. Predicates allocate no heap, while empty-directory scanning has linear entry work and kernel-dependent cost. linux/io.hpp reexports the classification enum as micron::node_types.

Related types and selection guide.

micron::posix::makedev(u32 major, u32 minor)

Defined in linux/io/inode.hpp, line 20.

inline posix::dev_t
makedev(u32 major, u32 minor)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • u32 major
  • u32 minor

Effects

Returns (static_cast<posix::dev_t>(minor & 0xffu)) | (static_cast<posix::dev_t>(major & 0xfffu) << 8) | (static_cast<posix::dev_t>(minor & ~0xffu) << 12) | (static_cast<posix::dev_t>(major & ~0xfffu) << 32).

Equivalent definition
inline posix::dev_t
makedev(u32 major, u32 minor)
{
  return (static_cast<posix::dev_t>(minor & 0xffu)) | (static_cast<posix::dev_t>(major & 0xfffu) << 8)
         | (static_cast<posix::dev_t>(minor & ~0xffu) << 12) | (static_cast<posix::dev_t>(major & ~0xfffu) << 32);
}

Return value

Evaluates the return expression reached in the equivalent definition. The return sites are listed in source order; preceding exits and enclosing branches determine which site is reached.

major(posix::dev_t dev)

micron::posix::is_inode_type(const char *path, stat_t &buf)

Defined in linux/io/inode.hpp, line 317.

template<node_types Tp>
inline bool
is_inode_type(const char *path, stat_t &buf)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • const char *path
  • stat_t &buf

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<node_types Tp>
inline bool
is_inode_type(const char *path, stat_t &buf)
{
  bool ok = (Tp == node_types::symlink) ? __impl::__lstat(path, buf) : __impl::__stat(path, buf);
  if ( !ok ) return false;
  return (buf.st_mode & __format_mask) == static_cast<i32>(Tp);
}

Return value

micron::posix::is_empty_dir(posix::fd_t fd)

Defined in linux/io/inode.hpp, line 1263.

inline bool
is_empty_dir(posix::fd_t fd)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • posix::fd_t fd

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
inline bool
is_empty_dir(posix::fd_t fd)
{
  posix::fd_t tmp{ static_cast<i32>(micron::syscall(SYS_dup, fd.fd)) };
  if ( !tmp ) return false;
  micron::syscall(SYS_lseek, tmp.fd, 0, posix::seek_set);
  alignas(posix::__linux_kernel_dirent64) char buf[512];      // 8-aligned: d_ino is u64 (ARM unaligned fault)
  bool empty = true;
  for ( ;; ) {
    max_t n = micron::syscall(SYS_getdents64, tmp.fd, buf, sizeof(buf));
    if ( n == 0 ) break;
    if ( n < 0 ) {
      if ( n == -error::interrupted ) continue;
      empty = false;
      goto done;
    }
    const usize nread = static_cast<usize>(n);
    usize pos = 0;
    while ( pos < nread ) {
      const posix::__linux_kernel_dirent64 *d = nullptr;
      const u16 reclen = posix::__dirent64_validate(buf, pos, nread, d);
      if ( reclen == 0 ) {
        empty = false;
        goto done;
      }
      pos += reclen;
      if ( !is_dot_entry(d->d_name) ) {
        empty = false;
        goto done;
      }
    }
  }
done:
  micron::syscall(SYS_close, tmp.fd);
  return empty;
}

Return value

Evaluates the return expression reached in the equivalent definition. The return sites are listed in source order; preceding exits and enclosing branches determine which site is reached.

micron::posix::is_mountpoint(const char *path)

Defined in linux/io/inode.hpp, line 1420.

inline bool
is_mountpoint(const char *path)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • const char *path

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
inline bool
is_mountpoint(const char *path)
{
  if ( path == nullptr || path[0] == '\0' ) return false;
  if ( path[0] == '/' && path[1] == '\0' ) return true;

  stat_t self{}, parent{};
  if ( !__impl::__stat(path, self) ) return false;

  char par[posix::path_max];
  usize i = 0;
  while ( path[i] && i < posix::path_max - 1 ) {
    par[i] = path[i];
    ++i;
  }
  par[i] = '\0';

  while ( i > 1 && par[i - 1] == '/' ) --i;
  while ( i > 1 && par[i - 1] != '/' ) --i;
  if ( i > 1 && par[i - 1] == '/' ) --i;
  if ( i == 0 ) {
    par[0] = '/';
    i = 1;
  }
  par[i] = '\0';

  if ( !__impl::__stat(par, parent) ) return false;
  return self.st_dev != parent.st_dev;
}

Return value

Evaluates the return expression reached in the equivalent definition. The return sites are listed in source order; preceding exits and enclosing branches determine which site is reached.

trueline 1423

falseline 1445
